# Skills · F · part 22

790 entries, few-shot-qa-fine-tuning to fg-web-a11y. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **few-shot-qa-fine-tuning**: Use when reproducing or comparing few-shot QA accuracy for T5 models on CommonsenseQA or OpenBookQA across adaptation objectives (Denoising, LM, Mixed) and prompting templates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/few-shot-qa-fine-tuning))
- **few-shot-quality-prompting**: Master guide for crafting prompts that make AI models produce professional-quality code and UI consistently. Trigger whenever the user asks about prompt engineering, improving AI output quality, building system prompts, few-shot examples, making AI write better code, prompt optimization, or says "how to prompt", "better results", "improve output", "stop getting slop". Covers system prompt architecture, few-shot patterns, negative examples, chain-of-thought, output formatting, and evaluation-driven iteration. ([mahmoud20138/Tradecraft](https://github.com/mahmoud20138/Tradecraft/tree/HEAD/plugins/tradecraft/skills/few-shot-quality-prompting) · ★18 · 2 copies)
- **few-shot-segmentation-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to perform semantic segmentation on 3D point clouds using only a few labeled examples per category. It probes how well learned point embeddings generalize to unseen shapes when supervision is extremely limited, testing both few-shot (few labeled shapes) and few-point (few labeled points per shape) scenarios. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ShapeNet segmentation dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports mIOU. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/few-shot-segmentation-eval) · ★3)
- **few-shot-task-sampling-from-mass-spectrometry-data**: Use when SCMS metabolomics data yield too few cells per phenotype for conventional deep learning and you need to build N-shot meta-learning tasks (e.g., primary vs metastatic melanoma classification from IGR39/IGR37 single-probe SCMS profiles). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/few-shot-task-sampling-from-mass-spectrometry-data))
- **few-shot-tool-invocation-prompting**: Use when an LLM agent must choose tools from a large registered inventory (e.g. a tool JSON catalog) and is prone to tool hallucination or erroneous calls caused by excessive contextual information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/few-shot-tool-invocation-prompting))
- **few-shot-transfer-learning-comparison**: Use when few (1-5) labeled single-cell mass spectrometry (SCMS) samples are available for a new cell group and you need to adapt a pre-trained meta-learning classifier and compare it with transfer-learning baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/few-shot-transfer-learning-comparison))
- **few-shot-ts-gen-eval**: Evaluates the ability of a generative model to produce high-fidelity time series data under extreme data scarcity (few-shot fine-tuning). It probes cross-domain generalization and robustness to varying sequence lengths and channel dimensions by comparing generated samples against real test data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ECG200, ETTh2, ETTm1, ETTm2, ILI, Weather, Synthetic sine wave, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports contextFID (c-FID). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/few-shot-ts-gen-eval) · ★3)
- **few-shot-uie-model-training-per-document-type**: Use when building a supervised information-extraction baseline over heterogeneous document types where labeled data is scarce — e.g. ~10 annotated samples per certificate type — and extraction fields differ per document class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/few-shot-uie-model-training-per-document-type))
- **few-shot-video-classification-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to classify video actions from a small number of labeled examples per class. It probes few-shot learning capabilities by measuring accuracy across thousands of randomly sampled episodes on standard video benchmarks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Kinetics, Something-Something V2, complete-Kinetics, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/few-shot-video-classification-eval) · ★3)
- **few-step-distillation-t2i**: Systematically adapt state-of-the-art distillation methods for T2I generation. Compare sCM (stabilized Consistency Models), MeanFlow, and IMM within unified framework. sCM excels at extreme few-step regimes (52.81% GenEval at 2 steps), MeanFlow achieves superior fidelity at 4 NFEs. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/few-step-distillation-t2i) · ★7)
- **few-tokens-matter-vlm-attacks**: Demonstrate that adversarial attacks on vision-language models need not target all tokens equally. Entropy-guided attacks identify high-entropy tokens (critical decision points) where perturbations have maximum impact, achieving comparable attack success with 80% fewer tokens targeted. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/few-tokens-matter-vlm-attacks) · ★7)
- **fewclue-eval**: Evaluates Chinese NLP models on few-shot learning across nine tasks, including single-sentence classification, sentence-pair classification, and machine reading comprehension. It tests the ability of pre-trained language models and few-shot prompting/fine-tuning methods to generalize with limited labeled data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on FewCLUE, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/fewclue-eval) · ★3)
- **fewer-faster-tests**: Auditing a project's test suite for agent-driven development: how long checking one change takes, which tests can fail for a reason and which cannot, what is missing as well as what is extra, whether a change runs only the tests it affects, and whether the agent can run the app and see the result. Lays out options with their trade-offs for the person to choose; changes no file. Use when tests or CI are slow, an agent keeps adding tests, before removing tests, when deciding how agents should test a project, or when asked to audit the tests or speed up CI. ([proxify-dev/agentic-kit](https://github.com/proxify-dev/agentic-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentic-engineering/skills/fewer-faster-tests) · ★7)

- **fewjoint-eval**: Evaluates few-shot joint language understanding by measuring a model's ability to simultaneously predict dialogue intent and extract slots from query sentences using only a small support set from unseen domains. - **fewmmbench-eval**: Evaluates multimodal large language models on few-shot learning capabilities across nine diverse tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on FewMMBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. - **fewshot-fmri-decoding-eval**: Evaluates few-shot learning methods for decoding brain activation maps from fMRI data.
